- **acervo-busca**: Busca e SÍNTESE no acervo local (jurisprudência, teses, doutrina, legislação) e — se a integração estiver ativa — no vault Obsidian, SEMPRE antes da web. Combina várias fontes numa resposta e CITA cada uma (seção `## Fontes`). Sinaliza lacunas (o que não há no acervo → pesquisa web) e contradições entre fontes. É READ-ONLY. Reforça o gate `verificacao-citacoes`: toda afirmação fica ancorada numa fonte verificável do acervo. Use quando o usuário pedir "o que temos no acervo sobre X", reunir teses/precedentes de um tema, ou fundamentar a partir da base interna. Port do padrão "Seeker". - **acervo-conexoes**: Tece o GRAFO do acervo/vault — cria e mantém wikilinks `[[...]]` ligando tese ↔ precedente ↔ caso ↔ pessoa ↔ dispositivo legal. Sugere as conexões ANTES de gravar, com a justificativa de cada uma; faz linking contextual (no corpo, não em listas soltas), bidirecional e seletivo (evita over-linking). Também audita o grafo (notas órfãs, clusters isolados) e propõe "notas-ponte". Use para conectar uma tese aos precedentes que a sustentam e aos casos onde foi aplicada. Requer a integração Obsidian ativa (ou opera com referências no `acervo/`). Port do padrão "Connector". É CONSERVADOR: nunca apaga, só **arquiva/sinaliza**; nunca faz merge automático, **sempre pergunta**. Produz um relatório de saúde. Reusa o `scripts/indexar-acervo.js`. - **adaptive-ui-audit-desktop-crosscutting-analyzer**: Desktop + cross-cutting analyzer for the adaptive-ui-audit skill. ALWAYS runs framework-specific adaptive rules, responsive-consistency, and anti-patterns; ALSO runs desktop window-resize (area D1) when the platform includes desktop. Reads the discovery artifact and writes up to four artifacts. Cross findings fold into scored areas by tagging (W*/N*/D1); the anti-pattern step also emits filter verdicts that downgrade/drop weak findings. Adversarial both ways, NO-INVENTED-OBSERVATIONS. ([mcampbellr/engineering-agents](https://github.com/mcampbellr/engineering-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/adaptive-ui-audit/agents/desktop-crosscutting-analyzer.md) · 3 copies)
- **adaptive-ui-audit-native-analyzer**: Native adaptive/responsive UI analyzer. Runs only for apps with platform=native (React Native / Expo AND Flutter). Covers safe-area & insets, keyboard/input visibility, adaptive layout (tablet/orientation/text-scaling), and touch ergonomics. Reads the discovery artifact and writes four area artifacts with severity + confidence classified findings and file:line evidence. Never applies web/DOM rules. Never invents runtime observations; a visual claim is OBSERVED only if a simulator/emulator render confirms it, else "Static analysis — verify on &lt;device/orientation/font setting&gt;" naming the tool. ([mcampbellr/engineering-agents](https://github.com/mcampbellr/engineering-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/adaptive-ui-audit/agents/native-analyzer.md) · 3 copies)
- **adaptive-ui-audit-orchestrator**: Single entry point for an in-session adaptive UI audit. Runs discovery, determines web/native/desktop scope, dispatches only the scope-relevant analysis subagents in dependency-ordered waves, validates artifacts between waves, and hands the manifest to the report-writer. Never reads source files or writes prose. Stops if no web, native, or desktop UI is detected. Never modifies application code. ([mcampbellr/engineering-agents](https://github.com/mcampbellr/engineering-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/adaptive-ui-audit/agents/orchestrator.md) · 3 copies)
- **adaptive-ui-audit-profiler**: Wave 0 profiler for the adaptive UI audit. Discovers the stack, the responsive/styling system, and safe-area/keyboard/orientation handling, and determines web/native/desktop scope (monorepo-aware) BEFORE any analysis, then writes the discovery artifact all downstream steps depend on. If no web, native, or desktop UI is found, records PLATFORMS=none so the orchestrator can stop.
